---
title: 8-stripe tartan setts (page 5)
date: 2023-03-18
weight: 8
---
1,454 setts across 1,386 tartans with 8 stripes, grouped by base-six **band pattern**, then by human-palette **stripe pattern**. Each band pattern is summarised first (popular first), then listed in full below. Each variant shows ΔT — its mean distance (OKLab ΔE) from the base colours — nearest first, with a stripe bar in the nearest human-palette colour names (hover a stripe to read it).
